# Project Red — Game Data

Auto-generated from the built ROM (`build/project-red.gba`) by `build/gen_doc_gamedata.py`.
Regenerate after any rebuild. Sections: [Wild Encounters](#wild-encounters), [Evolution Changes](#evolution-changes), [Boss Battles](#boss-battles), [Regular Trainers](#regular-trainers).

---

# Wild Encounters

Every location where wild Pokemon can be caught, with level ranges and per-encounter odds.
Odds are the standard FireRed slot rates (Grass 20/20/10.../1, Surf & Rock Smash 60/30/5/4/1,
Fishing split across Old/Good/Super Rod). Within a method, a species appearing in several
slots has its odds summed.

# Evolution Changes

Trade-based evolutions can't be done in single-player, so every one has been replaced with a
solo-achievable evolution. Nothing else about the evolution lines changed (same final forms).

**Former trade evolutions — now evolve by levelling up (Lv37):**

| Pokemon | Evolves into | Now |
|---|---|---|
| Boldore | Gigalith | Level 37 |
| Graveler | Golem | Level 37 |
| Gurdurr | Conkeldurr | Level 37 |
| Haunter | Gengar | Level 37 |
| Kadabra | Alakazam | Level 37 |
| Karrablast | Escavalier | Level 37 |
| Machoke | Machamp | Level 37 |
| Phantump | Trevenant | Level 37 |
| Pumpkaboo | Gourgeist | Level 37 |
| Shelmet | Accelgor | Level 37 |

**Former trade-with-item evolutions — now evolve by USING that item** (all sold at the Celadon evolution-item shop):

| Pokemon | Evolves into | Use item |
|---|---|---|
| Clamperl | Gorebyss | DeepSeaScale |

# Boss Battles

Gym leaders, Team Rocket, the Elite Four (and their rematches), the Champion (and rematch),
and every rival fight. Rival fights have three variants — the rival's team depends on which
starter YOU chose (he takes the one with the type advantage).
